- **game-physics-matter**: Add 2D rigid-body physics to a zero-build browser game with matter.js — gravity, collisions, stacks, drag-aim. Covers the pinned CDN script + Matter global, Engine/Composite/Bodies, stepping the engine on the fixed timestep, drawing bodies onto your own canvas, MouseConstraint drag, collisionStart events, and static/sleeping bodies. Use when the user says 'physics', 'matter.js', 'gravity', 'collisions', 'rigid body', 'slingshot', 'stacking', or 'pinball'. ([liammday/game-making-skills](https://github.com/liammday/game-making-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/game-physics-matter) · ★1)

- **game-pillars**: Set three to five design pillars for a game and use them to decide what gets built. Re-run at every milestone, and whenever a feature argument cannot be settled.
